# S6 城内十字街 · 闹市: the inner city: the map in words

Generated by `tools/scroll_map.mjs S6 text` from `map/strokes/S6.json` (stroke points on the scan) and `map/frames/S6.json`. Never edit this file: change the strokes or the frame and regenerate. Scan range u 2000–5600.

## How to read it

- **Station** is metres along the route (`S6.road.main`) from the datum, where the main street crosses the seam with S5 (u 5600): its last centre-line point, exactly S5's road end (scan 5600, 1250). Station -26.51 there, the station of S5.road.main's west end, so stations run on from the gate (more negative going west, into the city).. **+ is east** (picture right); **− is west**. The scroll is read from right to left, so the walk below starts at the east end.
- **Side** is metres north (N, away from the scroll's viewer) or south (S, toward the viewer) of the route's centre line. **H** is height above the ground.
- Directions: E = picture right, N = away from the viewer. "faces W" means the body faces west.
- Positions come from points measured on the strokes. "(est)" marks a point estimated because the strokes hide it (feet behind a rail, a corner behind a tree). Metres also rest on the frame's assumptions below.
- Painted figures are about 1.3× life size against the architecture; positions use foot points, so this doesn't move anyone.

## Frame and assumptions

- **F1** covers u 1850–5750: u = 8248 + 77·X + dx(u)·D; v = 1565 − 51.25·D − 77·H, where the receding direction dx(u) drifts along the scroll: 59.9 at u 1250, 50.4 at u 2000, 50.4 at u 2950, 54.4 at u 4170, 58.24 at u 5600 (linear between, constant beyond).
  - Derived: Continues S5's frame (map/frames/S5.json F1), so the joined world is one coordinate system: the same origin (S5.str.moat_bridge:base.sw, scan 8248, 1565), the same 77 px per metre across the street and in height, and the same 51.25 px of v per metre of depth. dx = 51.25 / |slope| for a receding (north-running) edge of slope dv/du.
  - Derived: Seam with S5 at u 5600: S5's receding direction there is 58.24 px per metre north (its gate value, kept constant west of u 7800 because S5 shows no reliable receding edge west of the gate), so the last knot is [5600, 58.24].
  - Derived: Seam with S7 at u 2000 (joined 2026-10-12, the round-2 join agent; map/README.md, One world across sections): S7's knots [1250, 59.9] and [2000, 50.4] (map/frames/S7.json F1) are copied here, because S6's entities that reach west of the seam (frame covers from u 1850) are placed with S7's drift. East of u 2000 nothing changes: dx was already a constant 50.4 there (west of the u 2950 knot). West of the seam dx now rises toward 59.9.
  - Derived: Knot [2950, 50.4], the west group (five edges, u 2190-3370): the incense compound's west range ridge (2190,310)->(2462,55), slope -0.94, dx 54.7; the incense shop's ridge (2660,335)->(2940,48), -1.025, dx 50.0; its east eave (2870,590)->(3245,200), -1.04, dx 49.3; the two long tables under its lean-to, west edges of their tops (3213,420)->(3290,337), -1.08, dx 47.5, and (3280,435)->(3367,347), -1.01, dx 50.7. Mean 50.4 at their mean u (~2950).
  - Derived: Knot [4170, 54.4], the middle group (three edges, u 3990-4346): the corner shop's ridge (3990,445)->(4235,165), -1.14, dx 44.8; its west eave, upper part (4060,240)->(4280,40), -0.91, dx 56.4; the west eave of the south-side house east of the inn (4134,1784)->(4346,1609), -0.83, dx 62.1. Mean 54.4.
  - Derived: East group (two edges, u 5272-5455), used as a check, not a knot: the parallel bars on the east side of the 正店 / 彩楼欢门 (5272,488)->(5432,340), -0.93, dx 55.4, and (5300,690)->(5455,520), -1.10, dx 46.7; mean 51.1 where the frame gives about 57.6 (see questions).
  - Derived: Not used: the square well's curb and platform sides (slopes -2.5 to -3.3: drawn almost from above), gable verges (they mix depth and height), the corner shop's lower west eave (it bends with the hip), the wagon's panels and shafts and the wine cart's casks (the vehicles are turning), and the 解 board's foot line (2050,1768)->(2120,1708), -0.86, dx 59.6 (only if the board stands square to the street).
  - Derived: The ten edges scatter by about +-5 px/m round 52 with no clear drift along the section; between the knots dx is linear, beyond them constant (S5's rule).
  - Assumed: East-west and height share S5's scale, 77 px per metre; depth is 51.25 px of v per metre everywhere on the ground. With them the street figures are about 1.3x life, as at the gate (adults 175-243 px, children 104-134 px, sedan bearers 156-182 px).
  - Assumed: Things far up the silk are painted smaller than the frame's one scale allows (see questions): positions along the street still come from foot points and base corners, but sizes read at the back of the silk are about 0.55-0.6 of what they would be at the street.
  - Assumed: Painted figure size is not a scale. Place by foot point.

- Support **ground**: H 0 m (street level; small undulations ignored. 'ground: on <prop>' marks goods whose base point is the ground under the stand, tub, block or counter they sit on).
- Support **zhengdian upper floor**: H 4.5 m (assumed: 孙羊正店's upper floor line (balustrade foot) is at v ~442 over u 4440-5650 and the street front (column feet, est) at v ~905; the ground under the balustrade is hidden, so floor height and set-back trade off: 6.0 m with no set-back, 4.5 m with 2.2 m (the upper storey's west end lines up with the ground floor's west column, and the ground-floor roof shows only ~110 px before the 彩楼's V-band), 3.4 m with 4 m. The middle reading is taken; the ground-floor eave is 2.6 m up (197 px), leaving a 1.9 m pent roof below the floor).
- Support **zhengdian table top**: H 5.25 m (derived: the upper floor (4.5 m) plus the table in its east room, whose dishes sit ~57 px (0.75 m) above the floor line).
- Support **inn upper floor**: H 3.4 m (assumed: the window sill of 久住王员外家's upper storey is at v ~1705 on its south face, and the ground below it is beyond the silk's edge; 3.4 m is the low reading of the 正店's floor. With it the inn's south wall foot falls at v ~1965 and its west gable apex (3810,1288) stands over the middle of its west wall, as a gable should).
- Support **well cover**: H 0.25 m (assumed: the square well's curb stands 10-15 px proud of its platform boards, which show 6-8 px of thickness: about 0.25 m; two water-drawers stand on the cover's back beam).
- Support **table top**: H 1.1 m (derived: the paper stall's table top stands 84 px above its leg feet (S6.prop.paper_stall_table_2395); painted furniture is drawn at the figures' 1.3x like them).
- Support **cart bed: S6.veh.wine_cart_3090**: H 0.75 m (derived: the driver's est foot on the front board (v 1500) against the cart's ground line under him (v ~1556, between its front (3090,1482) and its rear (2700,1682))).
- Support **cart bed: S6.veh.taiping_wagon_3600**: H 1.45 m (derived: the young man's est foot on the bed behind the front board (v 395) against the wheel's ground contact (3783,506)).
- Support **sedan**: H 0 m (the passenger's foot point is the ground under her seat (est); the sedan's floor height is the vehicle kit's choice).
- Support **shoulders**: H 0 m (a child carried on a man's shoulders: its foot point is the carrier's foot point; the ride height is the people page's choice).
- Support **carried on back**: H 1.25 m (derived: the child's est feet at the carrier's waist (v 1145), 97 px above the carrier's feet (v 1242)).

## Landmarks, east to west

- **main street (inside the gate, through the crossroads)** `S6.road.main`: stations −23.4 to −78.7; side 7.8 m N to 12.2 m S. The town's main east-west street, continuing S5's road west from the wine depot: past the front of 孙羊正店 (its 彩楼欢门, lamp-boxes and slatted railings; sedans and riders in the street), the butcher's shop and the corner shop with the storyteller's crowd, across the mouth of the cross street at the crossroads, past the incense shop and the paper-horse stall to the square well on its north margin. On the north side the open ground stops at the shop fronts; on the south side at the roofs of the houses along the silk's bottom edge (the inn 久住王员外家 and its neighbours), except between the 解 building (u ~2050-2165) and the inn's west wing (u ~3160), where it opens into a wide square that runs out over the silk's bottom edge. Note: No road edge is drawn as a line; edges are where the open ground stops. edge_n: the wall foot of the house behind the well (v ~800; the well stands on the street's north margin), the incense shop's south end (est v 790, behind the paper-horse stall's parasols, whose feet are at v ~850), its south-east corner (est 2908,790), then straight across the mouth of the cross street to the corner shop's south-west corner (est 3725,865), the corner shop, butcher's and 正店 fronts (column feet hidden by the crowd: est v 872, 880-885, 905; a gap of ~100 px between the butcher's and the 正店 at 4340-4441 holds only the slanting flag pole), and the wine depot's floor front (S5, est v 885-888). The 正店's slatted railings stand on the street ~95 px in front of its front (feet v ~1000). edge_s: the 解 building's north side and its slanted east board ((2045,1310) down to the silk's edge at (2165,1775); the building is S7's); open ground to the silk's edge (measured at v 1772-1785 along the section) from u ~2165 to the inn's west wing; the wing's west wall (est, along the receding direction from (3161,1775) to its NW corner (3501,1440)); then the north walls of the south-side houses, all hidden behind their own roofs, est at v 1440 to meet S5 (5600,1440). Nothing painted on the street stands lower than v ~1440 east of the wing (the boy at 3420,1395; trestles and a rider at 4800-5300, feet 1420-1440), so 1440 is the street's narrowest reading; the houses' real north walls may lie up to ~150 px lower (S5's house_5800 has porch posts with feet at v 1694-1758 under a top line at 1450): a question for the lead. Centre: through the traffic between the edges (walkers and porters at v 1100-1250 west of the crossroads; east of it midway between the fronts and v 1440, easing to S5's road end); its last point is exactly (5600,1250). The cross street S6.road.cross_2970 leaves it northward between u 2908 and 3725 (north edge).
- **south-side house behind the willows (west of S5's house_5800)** `S6.bld.house_south_4839`: stations −26.8 to −29.6; side 3.8 m S to 9.1 m S; footprint 2.2 m (front) × 5.1 m (deep); front faces N (est: sw, se, ne, nw). Small tiled house on the south side of the street, mostly hidden by the willows: its ridge runs east-west along v ~1450 from a curled ornament at its west end to where S5's big roof (house_5800) begins, with a band of round tile-ends below it. Note: Ridge (double line) v 1450-1465 from the ornament (4975,1420) to ~5145, where S5.bld.house_5800's west hip-gable end begins; tile-ends 4990-5140, v 1465-1500; west of it a lower band 4880-4980, v 1450-1510. Trestle frames stand on the street just above it (4800-5050, v 1340-1440: props). Base est with house_5800's depth (S5: v 1440 -> 1700), the ridge over its middle: the ornament (4975) and the ridge's east end (5145) then fall straight above the middle of its west and east walls.
- **south-side houses east of the inn (roofs running back)** `S6.bld.house_south_4300`: stations −28.2 to −33.2; side 4.0 m S to 14.5 m S; footprint 3.7 m (front) × 10.3 m (deep); front faces N (est: sw, se, ne, nw). Tiled roofs between the inn's east end and the willows along the bottom edge: a roof whose west eave runs diagonally back (north) up the silk from the silk's edge, a low roof at the silk's edge, and a roof edge under the willows; walls hidden. Possibly two or three small houses, recorded as one. Note: West eave running back: (4134,1784)->(4346,1609), slope -0.83 (dx ~62.1): DEPTH DIRECTION (med conf, checked on an overlay), with parallel tile rows above it (4184,1740)->(4352,1578); with a ~3 m eave its line lies along the inn's east wall, so this roof starts there. A low roof 4180-4370, v 1680-1775; a roof edge 4430-4520, v 1650-1775; south-slope tiles 4720-4830, v 1550-1700 (eave ~1645) under the willows. The small roof end with vertical tile rows at 4340-4420, v 1400-1470 near the street is more likely the inn's north-east part. Base est in ground metres: the inn's depth (v 1965 -> 1440), ~3.6 m wide, between the inn and S6.bld.house_south_4839.
- **inn 久住王员外家 (main hall, two storeys)** `S6.bld.inn_wang_yuanwai`: stations −31.8 to −43.1; side 4.4 m S to 15.4 m S; footprint 10.0 m (front) × 10.2 m (deep); heights se_eave 4.87 m; front faces N (est: sw, se, ne, nw). 久住王员外家, the inn: a two-storey hall on the south side of the street, seen from behind (south). A big hip-gable (歇山) roof with its west gable (king post and hanging-fish board 懸魚) turned to the viewer and its ridge running east to an upturned end; on the upper storey's south face, under a deep eave and frieze, a slatted window, the open bay where a scholar sits reading at a desk (士子苦读), a second open bay with a figure among books, and a little railed balcony at the east end; below, the ground floor's own roof with a small gable, cut by the silk's edge. Note: UPPER FLOOR: floor line (window sill, bottom of the window band) at v ~1705 over u 3690-4280, on the south face; the ground straight below it is beyond the silk's edge (the lower roof is cut by the silk at v ~1775, ~70 px below the sill), so the floor height cannot be measured here. Base est: south wall foot at v ~1965, i.e. the sill 3.4 m up as one reading of the 正店's floor (state this as an assumption); north wall at the street's south edge (v 1440): ~10.2 m deep, corners shifted by the receding direction (dx ~52). Check: the west gable apex (3810,1288) then lies straight above the middle of the west wall (ground u 3812), as a gable should. Roof: west gable apex (3810,1288), verges to (3600,1490) and (3930,1420); ridge from the apex east to ~(4300,1395), its east end turned up at (4330,1425); south eave (3950-4300) at v ~1560-1590. Upper storey band (frieze) v 1555-1600 (3570-4300); windows v 1600-1705: slatted 3690-3770, the scholar's bay 3860-3960 (the scholar seated, head at ~1650), second bay 4020-4100, balcony rail 4100-4200 at v ~1590. Lower roof with its own gable apex (3830,1680), 3680-4000, cut by the silk. DEPTH DIRECTION: none usable (the gable verge (3600,1490)->(3810,1288), slope -0.96, rises toward the apex and mixes depth and height). SCALE CUES (px): scholar's head and shoulders ~55 above the sill; slatted window ~80 x 100; frieze to sill ~145. Goes with S6.bld.inn_west_wing_3146.
- **slatted railing in front of 孙羊正店 (east, by the 香□ box)** `S6.str.railing_5190`: stations −34.5 to −35.8; side 4.2 m N to 4.1 m N. Row of short vertical slats under a rail in front of the 正店's east door beside the 香□ lamp-box, probably the same kind of barrier fence as at the west door. Note: Top rail and slats seen at 5190-5300, v ~870-905; feet est at v ~995 (the west fence's height). Could also be the balustrade of a counter at the door.
- **孙羊正店 (the 孙羊 licensed wine house)** `S6.bld.sunyang_zhengdian`: stations −34.8 to −47.5; side 14.4 m N to 5.1 m N; footprint 12.0 m (front) × 8.5 m (deep); heights sw_eave 2.56 m, se_eave 2.6 m; front faces S (est: sw, se, ne, nw, se_eave). 孙羊正店, the big licensed wine house, two storeys. Ground floor: an open street front on round columns under a long tiled lean-to roof, with doors behind the 正店 and 香□ lamp-boxes, drinkers at tables, wine jars and two big bottle-shaped decorations at the doors; its whole front is masked by the 彩楼欢门 and fenced by low slatted railings. Upper storey (the drinking floor), behind a balustrade: lattice windows and open bays with drinkers at tables, under a big hip-gable roof with a heavy rolled ridge, a curled ornament and a small gable at its east end, and upturned eave corners at both ends; the upper storey runs east past the ground floor, behind the wine depot (S5), to u ~5700. Note: UPPER FLOOR: floor line (balustrade foot; rail top v ~420) at v ~442 over u 4440-5650; the street front below it (column feet, est) is at v ~905, and the ground straight below the balustrade is hidden. Readings: if the balustrade stood straight above the street front the floor would be 6.0 m up; the upper storey's west end (lattice from 4560, balustrade from 4440) lines up with the ground floor's west column (4441) for a set-back of ~0-2.2 m (ground v ~905-792, floor 6.0-4.5 m); a 4 m set-back (ground v ~700) gives 3.4 m. The ground-floor roof shows only ~110 px (eave 705 -> ~600) before the 彩楼's V-band, which also argues for a small set-back. Upper front: lattice windows 4570-4800 and 4900-5300 (v 255-420), open east bays 5300-5650 with a drinker at (5515,360-420), lattice window 5580-5640, east post 5700-5710. Upper eave (tile-end discs) v ~250 (4560-4800), ~275 (4880-5180), ~285-290 (5290-5650), turned up to ~(5700,250); rolled ridge v ~40-55 from ~4650 to the east ornament (5630,30); east gable 5600-5660, v 40-200, with a lower skirt roof 5580-5770, v 160-260; west end a hip from (4650,25) down to the turned-up corner (4515,175). Ground floor: eave v 705-712 (4440-5350), its roof rising to v ~600; columns (feet hidden, est v 905): 4441 (round, lashed: also the 彩楼's west pole), 4555-4570 and 4590-4600 (door posts by the 正店 box), 4760-4775, 4800-4815, 5265-5290 (round, capital at v 785-800), 5340-5355; its east end meets the wine depot's west post (5395-5410). Bottle-shaped decorations 4400-4440 and 5030-5070, v 770-880 (props). Base: front est v 905; back est 8.5 m deep (dx ~54), to the S5 back hall's front. DEPTH DIRECTION: the parallel members on the east side (see the 彩楼): (5272,488)->(5432,340), slope -0.93 (dx ~55.4), and (5300,690)->(5455,520), slope -1.10 (dx ~46.7). SCALE CUES (px): ground-floor eave ~195 above the est feet; columns 15-20 thick; bays 35-120 (4441-4562 = 121, door 4562-4595, 4808-5277 has no visible column); upper lattice windows ~70 x 140; balustrade ~22 tall; drinkers upstairs ~60-70 (half hidden). COMPLEX: with S6.str.cailou_huanmen_4430, the railings S6.str.railing_*, and probably S5.bld.back_hall_5790 (it abuts the upper storey's east end at u ~5700) with the railed stair at its west end. MERGE (from t5): also painted on its ground-floor front, as parts of this building: a dark cloth banner hung at u 4971-5006, v 756-804, and a shop counter inside at u 4730-4810, v 805-840.
- **彩楼欢门 (flowered scaffold gateway) of 孙羊正店** `S6.str.cailou_huanmen_4430`: stations −35.9 to −46.5; side 6.2 m N to 4.9 m N; footprint 10.8 m (front) × 0.5 m (deep); heights sw_top 9.42 m, se_top 9.61 m (est: sw, se, ne, nw, sw_top). The flowered scaffold gateway across the whole front of 孙羊正店: lashed poles (the west corner pole round and bound with rope lashings) rise from the street front past the ground-floor eave to the upper storey's eave, their tops tied with flower bunches, sprays and streamers; between them a lattice of cross-bars and diagonal braces forms a broad shallow V across the middle, hung with flowers and red streamers, and on its east side parallel bars run back toward the building. Lanterns: round 栀子灯 with cross-pattern frames on posts at the doors and one high in the middle, and a cube lantern above the 正店 box. At its feet stand the lamp-boxes 正店, 孫□ and 香□ (see note). Note: Read from the scroll's strokes only (the AI restyle turns it into a second tier of balconies). West pole 4432-4450, seen from v ~380 down to ~880 with lashings at v 735-760 (it doubles as the 正店's west column); flower-topped poles at 4460, 4495, 4530 rise to v 170-200; others at ~4880-4900 and ~4990-5010; the east-most tall pole at 5292-5302 with a pointed, flagged top at v ~170; a slender post at 5340-5355 (v 680-900). Main horizontals: a top beam at v ~430-450 (4440-5300, just under the upper balustrade) and a lower beam at v ~700-710 (along the ground-floor eave). V-braces: (4600,600) down to (4800,690) and (5170,600) down to (4980,690). Lanterns: round at (4655,735) and (5105,745), each on a post (4645-4660 and 5075-5085, v ~760-900), round at (4845,655) on the scaffold, cube at (4525,725). Lamp-boxes (box with a small cap, standing on the ground at the doors: tile agents' props): 正店 4482-4553, v 790-900 (both characters clear); 孫□ 4650-4695, v 795-880 (孫 clear, the second hidden by heads; 羊 per sections.md); 香□ 5130-5195, v 790-900 (香 clear, the second hidden by a headdress; 醪 per sections.md). Base: a strip ~0.6 m deep on the 正店's column line (est, feet hidden). DEPTH DIRECTION: the parallel bars on its east side, (5272,488)->(5432,340) (slope -0.93) and (5300,690)->(5455,520) (slope -1.10), run back toward the building (its east face, or the upper storey's east side; checked on an overlay). SCALE CUES (px): poles ~12-18 thick; overall height ~730 (915 -> 175-190, about 9.4 m at 77 px/m); lamp-box 正店 71 wide x 110 tall; round lanterns ~45-55 across.
- **slatted railing in front of 孙羊正店 (middle)** `S6.str.railing_4610`: stations −41.9 to −42.9; side 3.5 m N to 3.4 m N. A second stretch of the same slatted barrier fence, east of the gap in front of the 正店 door. Note: Slats 4610-4700 from v ~895 down to ~1000 (feet est). Its east end is lost in the crowd.
- **west wing of the inn 久住王员外家** `S6.bld.inn_west_wing_3146`: stations −42.3 to −50.5; side 5.2 m S to 12.5 m S; footprint 7.6 m (front) × 6.8 m (deep); front faces N (est: sw, se, ne, nw). West wing of the inn, seen from behind (its street front faces north): a lower tiled roof whose ridge runs east-west with a curled ornament at its west end and a small hip-gable (歇山) gable rising above it; walls hidden. The inn's signboard 久住王員外家 (a top cell with 久住 in two small characters, then 王 員 外 家) stands just west of it, its foot hidden by the willows. Note: Ridge v ~1420 (3335-3688), west ornament (3324,1410), which lies straight above the middle of its est west wall; small gable 3430-3476, apex ornament (3453,1384); tiles down to an eave at v ~1530-1560. SIGN 3245-3300, v 1298-1505 (all six characters clear); it may stand on its own post (tile agents' prop); in ground terms it stands ~1.8 m west of this wing. A boy stands on the street just above its ridge (feet ~3420,1395). Base est in ground metres: north wall at the street's south edge (v 1440), south wall at the silk's edge (v 1790, the eave ~245 px above it), the inn's west wall as its east side; corners shifted by the receding direction (dx ~52). DEPTH DIRECTION: none reliable (the roof's lower edge (3465,1546)->(3612,1493), slope -0.36, is a curved eave).
- **slatted railing in front of the 正店 west door** `S6.str.railing_4412`: stations −43.5 to −45.4; side 3.4 m N to 3.2 m N. Low barrier fence of close-set vertical slats under a top rail, standing on the street in front of 孙羊正店's west door, just below the 正店 lamp-box; a gap (the way in) separates it from the next stretch at 4610. Note: Slats 4412-4565 from the top rail (v ~885-890; a second rail at ~905) down to v ~1000-1003. SCALE CUES: ~115 px tall; slats ~6-8 px apart. Gap 4565-4610 (a man stands in it). Stands ~1.9 m in front of the 正店's column line (est v 905).
- **孙羊店 butcher's shop** `S6.bld.butcher_shop_sun`: stations −48.1 to −50.9; side 8.3 m N to 5.3 m N; footprint 3.3 m (front) × 3.0 m (deep); heights se_eave 3.75 m; front faces S (est: sw, se, ne, nw). Butcher's shop of 孙羊店 east of the corner shop: a tiled front roof (vertical tile rows) under a straight ridge, a lattice frieze and a dark valance under the eave, a pale wall panel at its west end and an open front hung with pale cloth panels; the long white flag 孫羊店 hangs in front of it from a pole slanting across its front, and a big parasol shades the street before it (the flag's pole and the parasol are tile agents' props). Note: Ridge v ~425 (4085-4335); eave v 590-600 (4100-4330); frieze 600-640, valance 640-700 (4200-4330); pale wall 4100-4180, v 560-850. FLAG 4130-4205, v 590-720: 孫 羊 店, clear, hung from a pole running straight from (4085,545) at this roof's west end down to (4445,835) at the 正店's west column (where its foot probably stands). Front feet est v ~880 behind the parasol (4190-4400, v 740-860). Back est 3 m deep; it may instead be the corner shop's east lean-to (its ridge starts where the corner shop's east slope ends). Between it and the 正店 (4340-4440) only slanting members and the flag pole show (an alley?). SCALE CUES: eave ~285 above the est feet; flag 75 x 130. MERGE: the meat counter, chopping block, hanging pork and the butcher himself (t4: S6.prop.meat_counter_3856, S6.prop.chopping_block_3963, S6.prop.hanging_meat_3857, S6.fig.butcher_3920) stand under the corner shop's eave at u 3788-3980, so the pork is sold from S6.bld.corner_shop_3725; this building carries the 孫羊店 flag before it. The two may be one shop (孙羊店), this its east lean-to (as the structures pass suspected); the name is kept.
- **corner shop at the crossroads (storyteller in front)** `S6.bld.corner_shop_3725`: stations −51.1 to −55.7; side 16.8 m N to 5.4 m N; footprint 4.3 m (front) × 11.1 m (deep); heights sw_eave 4.09 m, nw_eave 3.44 m; front faces S (est: sw, se, ne, nw). Shop on the north-east corner of the crossroads, where the storyteller holds his crowd: a long hall running back (north) along the east side of the cross street under a hip-gable (歇山) roof, its small gable facing the main street above a front roof whose south-west corner turns up, beast ornaments on the hips and a lattice frieze under the eave; open front on posts with a counter inside; a small signboard with three or four illegible characters (□□□□) hangs under the eave by its west post. Its long west eave runs back up the cross street almost to the top of the silk. Note: Front eave v ~600 (3730-4065) with a lattice frieze 600-650; SW eave corner turned up at (3720,548), hip ornaments at (3800,515) and (3880,465); small south gable 3940-4020, v 450-530, apex (3990,445). DEPTH DIRECTION: ridge (3990,445)->(4235,165), slope -1.14 (dx ~44.8); west eave upper part (4060,240)->(4280,40), slope -0.91 (dx ~56.4) (its lower part (3720,548)->(3890,440) bends with the hip skirt: not used). Posts 3760-3775 (v 650 to ~860), ~3870 (v 650-760), a tall dark post 4065-4090 (v 500-870) at its east end (or the butcher's west post). Counter top v 720-745 (3780-4000). SIGN 3772-3800, v 648-707, hung below the frieze (characters not readable). Base: front feet est v ~865-872 (hidden by the listeners); NW foot = the west eave's north end (4280,40) + 265 px (the front's eave height) = (4280,305): ~10.9 m deep; NE by the same shift (the east side is hidden; the butcher's roof may be this hall's east lean-to). SCALE CUES (px): eave 265 above the front feet; posts ~15 wide; counter ~220 long; listeners 110-140 tall. MERGE: its counter (3780-4000) is the 孫羊店 meat counter of the tile maps (S6.prop.meat_counter_3856 with the chopping block, hanging pork and the butcher), and the storyteller stands just in front of it; the 孫羊店 flag hangs before the next building east (S6.bld.butcher_shop_sun), which may be this shop's east lean-to.
- **top street (behind the incense shop, in front of the cloth shop)** `S6.road.top_2814`: stations −51.5 to −79.8; side 21.4 m N to 14.6 m N. An east-west street at the very top of the silk, where the cross street ends: people and a handcart with a mule behind the incense shop's north gable, and the cloth shop and the 杨家 clinic fronting it on its north side; its west part runs behind the incense compound and out of the silk at the top-left. Note: Ground is seen at (3050-3250, v 80-165) behind the incense shop's north gable (two men at a bench or rail, a man with a load) and around the cart at 3260-3420, v 30-160. edge_n: the cloth shop's and clinic's post feet (v 180-197); west of the cloth shop's west end the ground reaches higher (v ~80) and the silk's top edge. edge_s: the north range of the incense compound (est v 286), the incense shop's north gable (est 3245,440), across the cross street's mouth, the corner shop's north end (est). Its east end is hidden behind the corner shop's roof and the big tree at 4150-4500; its course beyond is unknown.
- **cross street at the crossroads (running north)** `S6.road.cross_2970`: stations −55.4 to −67.5; side 18.3 m N to on the centre line. The street that leaves the main street northward at the crossroads, between the incense shop's open east front (its west side) and the corner shop's long west side (its east side); two four-mule wagons swing round the corner on it and a porter with baskets crosses it. It runs straight back up the silk and ends at the top street, in front of the cloth shop and the 杨家 clinic. Note: Both sides are receding lines (the incense shop's east eave, slope -1.04, and the corner shop's west eave and ridge, -0.91 / -1.14), so it runs due north. Its walls' feet are hidden: edge_w = the incense shop's east wall (est 240 px below its east eave, the eave height measured on the corner shop's front); edge_e = the corner shop's west wall (est 265 px below its west eave). About 11.5 m wide (820 px of u at the mouth). The centre starts on the main street's centre line (2970,1178) and is drawn along the receding direction (dx ~52). It meets the main street's north edge between (2908,790) and (3725,865). South of the main street there is no built edge: the ground between the 解 building and the inn's west wing is an open square running out over the silk's edge, so the street may continue south across it, but nothing marks it. North: it ends at the top street (S6.road.top_2814), across which the cloth shop and the clinic front it (their post feet v 180-197); it leaves the silk only through that street. Tables and a lattice cabinet under the incense shop's lean-to stand on its west margin (3200-3330).
- **杨家 clinic (杨家应症)** `S6.bld.clinic_yang`: stations −59.2 to −62.0; side 22.5 m N to 19.1 m N; footprint 2.7 m (front) × 3.0 m (deep); front faces S (est: se, ne, nw). Shop at the top of the silk east of the cloth shop, facing south onto the top street: tiled roof cut by the silk's top edge, a pale frieze board under the eave, an open front with a counter on the west side and a man standing at the front; the tall vertical signboard 楊家應症 hangs on its east corner post. Note: Eave v ~40-45 (3850-4090); frieze v 45-80, lintel ~95-100 (3900-4000); posts 3900 (v 60-175) and the east corner post 4100-4110 (v 0-~200) carrying the SIGN 4100-4145, v 0-140: 楊 家 應 症, all four clear. 杨大夫经诊 (sections.md) is not seen on the scan. The man at its front (4010-4050) stands ~100 px tall. Back corners est (3 m deep). DEPTH DIRECTION: none drawn.
- **王家 cloth shop (匹帛铺)** `S6.bld.cloth_shop_wang`: stations −62.3 to −68.9; side 22.6 m N to 19.4 m N; footprint 4.8 m (front) × 3.0 m (deep); heights sw_eave 1.08 m, se_eave 1.12 m; front faces S (est: se, ne, nw, se_eave). Long open-fronted shop at the top of the silk facing south onto the top street, where the cross street ends: a dark, densely tiled roof cut by the silk's top edge, six small square plaques with one character each hung along its eave (illegible: □□□□□□), posts on bases and long counters under the eave. At its west end stands its tall signboard (王家羅明匹帛鋪 per sections.md; legible □錦疋帛 with 鋪 in a separate lower box) on a stand of its own. Note: A dark eave fascia band from the silk's top down to v ~95-100 (3450-3850) (sw_eave/se_eave = its lower edge); the plaques, ~20 px square at v 48-78, hang on it, centred at u ~3488, 3550, 3612, 3680, 3745, 3810 (not readable at this resolution); posts 3505-3515 (foot 3510,183) and 3700-3710 (foot ~188); counters 3540-3640 (v 105-160) and 3640-3740 (v 100-170); its east end meets the clinic at ~3850. Back corners est (3 m deep), roof top beyond the silk. SIGN on its own stand (a tile agents' prop): board 3415-3475, v 0-120 (first visible character cut by the silk edge, then one read as 錦 (unclear; sections.md has 明), 疋, 帛), lower box 3430-3470, v 85-120 (鋪), frame and feet to v ~150. SCALE CUES (px): posts ~88 tall from the fascia's lower edge to their feet, ~10 thick, 195 apart; plaques 20; the man at the clinic next door stands ~100 tall, taller than these posts: the buildings at the top of the silk are drawn small (front-street eaves stand 195-285 above their feet). DEPTH DIRECTION: none drawn.
- **pole scaffold (small 彩楼) on the incense shop's east front** `S6.str.cailou_incense_2895`: stations −66.7 to −67.3; side 12.0 m N to 7.5 m N; footprint 0.3 m (front) × 4.5 m (deep); heights sw_top 6.01 m, nw_top 4.58 m; front faces E (est: sw, se, ne, nw). A decorated scaffold of tall thin poles with spear-like finials along the incense shop's east front at its south-east corner, on the cross street: the poles rise past the shop's east eave and are tied by a lattice of cross-bars running back along the eave, hung with small ornaments (tassels or goods); a small 彩楼 like the 正店's, the 劉家 signboard standing just north-east of it. Note: Poles at u ~2895 (top v ~340), ~2930-2960, ~2985-2990 (top ~260), ~3045 (top ~220), ~3100 (top ~220), ~3117 (top ~260); lattice cross-bars along the east eave from about (2880,560) up to (3110,300). Feet est on the shop's east wall-foot line (S6.bld.incense_shop_liu: (2908,790) -> (3245,440), slope -1.04): at u 2895 v ~803, at u 3117 v ~573, so the scaffold stands along the east front, about 4.5 m of it from the south-east corner. Heights about 6.0 m (west) and 4.1 m (east) on the frame. Added in the merge (lead's second pass): t2 left the poles to the structures agent, which named them in the shop's note as props. Whether they carry goods (a display rack) or are a small 彩楼欢门 is open; built as one structure either way.
- **刘家 incense shop (香药铺)** `S6.bld.incense_shop_liu`: stations −67.0 to −74.8; side 14.6 m N to 7.6 m N; footprint 7.6 m (front) × 6.8 m (deep); heights se_eave 3.1 m, ne_eave 3.12 m; front faces E (est: sw, se, ne, nw). The 刘家 incense and perfume shop, the east range of the U-shaped compound: a long tiled hall running back (north) along the west side of the cross street, its ridge receding to a gabled north end with a ridge ornament; its east eave carries a lattice fascia board with two or three illegible characters near its south end. Under a dark matted lean-to roof along its east side the open shop front faces the cross street, with long tables and a lattice cabinet; the tall vertical signboard 劉家上色沉檀揀□ (last character hidden behind a hat; 香 by scholarship) stands at its south-east front corner. A row of tall poles hung with small goods stands along its east eave. Note: Ridge (2660,335)->(2940,48), slope -1.025 (dx ~50.0); east eave (2870,590)->(3245,200), slope -1.04 (dx ~49.3): both DEPTH DIRECTION. North gable verge from the ridge end (2940,48) down to the NE eave corner (3245,200): the east half-width is 305/77 = 4.0 m and the ridge 2.0 m above the eave. Base est: wall feet 240 px below the eave (the corner shop's front shows 265, the 正店 ground floor ~195), so SE (2908,790) and NE (3245,440); west wall 3.5 m west of the ridge. Its south end is hidden (the paper-horse stall's parasols and a pale cloth awning stand in front of it). Lean-to: dark roof from the east eave out to ~(3330,330), over tables at 3200-3330, v 360-560 and a lattice cabinet at 3240-3330, v 290-370 (props). SIGN 3133-3170, v 345-565: 劉 家 上 色 沉 檀 揀 legible, the eighth character behind a hat; its foot (~3150,570) is just east of the est wall line, so it may stand on its own post (tile agents' prop). Fascia inscription at ~2945-2990, v 540-590 (illegible). Tall poles with hanging goods at 2900-3150, v 200-560 (props). SCALE CUES (px): sign 37 x 220; table rows ~80-100 long, legs ~50; fascia band ~25.
- **north range of the incense compound (top of the silk)** `S6.bld.north_range_2283`: stations −71.3 to −79.8; side 17.5 m N to 14.3 m N; footprint 8.5 m (front) × 3.0 m (deep); heights se_ridge 5.09 m; front faces S (est: sw, se, ne, nw). North range of the U-shaped compound: a tiled roof whose ridge runs east-west between the ridge ornaments at the compound's two back corners, its south slope of vertical tile rows seen between the west range and the incense shop, ending in a small inner eave corner over a narrow light-well. Note: Ridge (2470,55)->(2940,48), level; south-slope tile rows end at v ~190-200 (2560-2800), inner eave corner ornament at (2660,190). Base est: placed behind (north of) both ranges' north ends so footprints do not overlap; the drawn ridge then stands over its south wall line (the ranges are one connected roof and only its ridges are drawn, so the split is a choice). The ridge's east end (2940,48) is straight above se, 5.1 m up. Above the ridge, at the silk's top (2560-2720, v 0-50), more structure: S6.bld.far_building_2561.
- **square well (方井) with its 田-shaped cover** `S6.str.square_well_2000`: stations −74.3 to −77.2; side 5.8 m N to 4.3 m N; footprint 2.3 m (front) × 1.6 m (deep). The square well on the street's north margin at the west end of the section: a low wooden curb with a 田-shaped plank cover (a cross of bars dividing the opening into four, water strokes in the openings), set in the middle of a wide plank platform whose boards run east-west; men draw water at it with ropes, and tall hooped wooden buckets stand on the platform's west end. Note: Base = the curb/cover. Curb front edge v ~955 (2000-2175), back edge v ~880 (2030-2205); cross bars at v ~915 and u ~2100-2110. PLATFORM (part of this structure, around the curb): back edge v ~855 from ~1995 (behind the buckets) to 2282; front edge v ~997 from ~1955 to 2240; east side (2240,998)->(2282,858). Its boards show 6-8 px of thickness at the front and the curb stands ~10-15 px proud of them: both low, well under knee height. SEAM: the curb's centre is (2102,917) and the platform's (2118,927), east of u 2000, so the well is S6's; only the platform's west end (u ~1955-2000) lies on the S7 side. SCALE CUES (px): curb 175 wide at the front, 75 deep in v; platform ~285 wide, ~140 deep in v; buckets 45-55 wide, 55-65 tall; the men at the well ~100-110 tall. DEPTH DIRECTION: platform east side (2240,998)->(2282,858) (slope -3.3) and curb east side (2175,955)->(2205,880) (slope -2.5) are far steeper than every building edge (-0.8 to -1.15): the well is drawn almost from above; use them with low weight or not at all.
- **house behind the square well** `S6.bld.house_well_2020`: stations −74.6 to −78.4; side 9.9 m N to 7.3 m N; footprint 3.8 m (front) × 2.5 m (deep); heights sw_eave 1.75 m; front faces S (est: sw, se, ne, nw). Single-storey house behind the square well: a tiled roof (vertical tile rows; ridge hidden by willow crowns) with round tile-ends along its eave, over a plain blank wall facing the street; the men at the well stand in front of its foot. Its roof runs on east behind the big willow's trunk. Note: Eave v 665-672 with tile-end discs from 2020 to 2250 and the same roof seen through the willow at 2270-2450, v 530-670; blank wall 2020-2250, v 672-800; a horizontal line at v ~795-800 (1915-2240) read as its wall foot (or the top of a low yard wall in front). East end est at u 2310, where the incense shop's south end begins. A pole stands at its east end (2245-2255, v 650-900: a prop if free-standing). West of 2000 a small dark sloping roof (1880-1960, v 735-805) belongs to S7. Back est 2.5 m deep. SCALE CUES: eave ~130 px above the foot (1.3 x the well men's ~100 px). DEPTH DIRECTION: none drawn.
- **west range of the incense compound** `S6.bld.wing_west_2053`: stations −74.7 to −79.8; side 14.4 m N to 9.8 m N; footprint 5.0 m (front) × 4.5 m (deep); front faces E (est: sw, se, ne, nw). West range of a compound under one U-shaped tiled roof (with S6.bld.north_range_2283 and the incense shop): its ridge runs back (north) from behind the big willows to the ridge ornament at the north-west corner, where it turns east into the north range's ridge; tiles on both slopes, walls hidden by the willows. Note: Ridge (2190,310)->(2462,55), the corner ornament at (2462,55): DEPTH DIRECTION, slope -0.94 (dx ~54.7). From the ornament a valley line runs down-right to the inner eave corner (2660,195) between this range's east slope and the north range's south slope. West slope tiles 2120-2330, v 230-450. Base est in ground metres: ridge ~5.1 m up (as the incense shop's), so the ridge lies over X ~-89.8; 5 m wide; north end at the incense shop's north gable line; south end just behind the house behind the well. Facing assumed east (toward the light-well between the ranges).
- **building beyond the compound (top edge)** `S6.bld.far_building_2561`: stations −78.2 to −80.3; side 22.9 m N to 20.3 m N; footprint 2.0 m (front) × 2.5 m (deep); front faces S (est: sw, se, ne, nw). Fragment of a building at the silk's top edge beyond the compound's north range: diagonal timbers, a small pale gable and a tiny signboard with two or three illegible characters (□□). Note: Strokes at 2560-2720, v 0-50: diagonal members (2560,50)->(2620,0) and parallels, a pale triangle (gable) at 2680-2720, v 10-50, a small board with characters at 2585-2620, v 5-35. Base est on the top street's north side (feet hidden behind the north range). Could also be a stall.
- **far house above the bamboo (top left)** `S6.bld.far_house_1859`: stations −80.3 to −88.7; side 21.4 m N to 18.2 m N; footprint 8.4 m (front) × 3.0 m (deep); front faces S (est: sw, se, ne, nw). Long tiled house at the very top-left of the silk, behind the bamboo groves: its roof (diagonal tile rows) cut by the silk's top edge, an eave at v ~100, and small gable ends with ornaments at both ends (u ~1825 and ~2330); walls hidden by the bamboo. Note: Roof tiles v 0-100 from ~1820 to 2330; end ornaments/gables at 1820-1830 (v 40-100) and 2320-2340 (v 40-110). Front est just behind the bamboo and the low fence (v ~227), 3 m deep, shifted by the receding direction (so its corners sit east of the painted roof ends). SEAM: it straddles S7 (west end at u ~1825, main point at u 1859); most of it is in S6.
- **low fence among the bamboo (top left)** `S6.str.fence_far_2000`: stations −84.6 to −86.1; side 18.2 m N to 18.0 m N. Low fence of horizontal boards among the bamboo groves at the top-left of the section, in front of the far house S6.bld.far_house_1859. Note: Horizontal strokes 2000-2120, v 180-240, with a small round object at (2110,195). Could be the top of a low shed instead.

## Everything, east to west

### Stations −20.0 to −25.0

- **man holding a carrying pole against his load** `S6.fig.pole_man_5578`: station −22.8, 4.4 m S; 4.5 m E of south-side house behind the willows (west of S5's house_5800); standing; faces W. Man with his hair in a knot and a light jacket, face in profile to the west, holding a shoulder pole in both hands before his chin; its east end rests on his load (a bundle of upright sticks and a round flat object) standing beside S5.fig.roof_watcher_5712. Nearest: crouching dark-clad figure behind the ridge 1 m W, groom at the horse's head 3.2 m W, man in a light robe riding the dark horse 3.3 m W. [est: foot; part: below v ≈ 1385 hidden by the low south-side roof] Note: Pole from his hands (5550, 1357) to the load at (5623, 1345). The load (u 5620-5670) stands in S5's range, beside S5.fig.roof_watcher_5712: a bundle of about five upright sticks (u 5623-5643, top v 1263) with a round flat object (round basket lid, sieve or drum, u 5637-5670, v 1317-1380) leaning on it; its base is hidden, est (5645, 1485). Foot est from a standing height of ≈175 px (he may be resting half-seated; his head is 34 px lower than the roof watcher's).
- **crouching dark-clad figure behind the ridge** `S6.fig.crouching_dark_5515`: station −23.8, 4.3 m S; 3.5 m E of south-side house behind the willows (west of S5's house_5800); crouching; faces W. A figure in dark clothes crouching behind the low south-side roof west of the pole man, back to the viewer, head bowed and one arm forward; or a dark sack set down. Nearest: man holding a carrying pole against his load 1 m E, groom at the horse's head 2.4 m SW, man in a light robe riding the dark horse 2.4 m W. [est: foot; identification low; part: below v ≈ 1395 hidden by the roof] Note: Dark crumpled shape u 5487-5545, v 1347-1400 with an arm-like curve at (5487-5517, 1357-1370); no face is drawn. Its height (≈130 px above the est ground) suits a crouching man better than a sack, and the restyle also paints a crouching man, but it may be a dark bundle.
- **bare bush-tree at the bottom edge (east)** `S6.tree.bare_shoots_5105`: station −24.1, 10.3 m S; 3 m SE of south-side house behind the willows (west of S5's house_5800); about 3.7 m tall; crown 2.4 m wide. Second low tree of long, thin, leafless shoots rising from below the silk's bottom edge, its shoots crossing in front of the tiled roof east of the signboard. Nearest: bare bush-tree at the bottom edge (west) 1.2 m W, groom at the horse's head 4.9 m N, tall standing signboard behind the south-side ridge 5 m NW. [est: base; identification med; part: trunk below the silk edge (v ≈ 1773)] Note: Shoots converge toward u ≈ 5087-5123 at the silk edge; the eastmost shoots cross the roof tiles at u 5150-5240.

### Stations −25.0 to −30.0

- **bare bush-tree at the bottom edge (west)** `S6.tree.bare_shoots_5012`: station −25.4, 10.4 m S; 2.1 m SE of south-side house behind the willows (west of S5's house_5800); about 3.9 m tall; crown 2.21 m wide. Low tree or pollard of many long, thin, leafless shoots fanning up from below the silk's bottom edge in front of the south-side roofs; its trunk is cut off by the silk edge. Nearest: bare bush-tree at the bottom edge (east) 1.2 m E, tall standing signboard behind the south-side ridge 4.4 m N, head behind the ridge beside the signboard 4.6 m NW. [est: base; identification med; part: trunk below the silk edge (v ≈ 1773)] Note: Shoots converge toward u ≈ 4994-5050 at the silk edge. The twig mass u 4880-5250 reads as two or three such trees: this one, S6.tree.bare_shoots_5105, and one converging at u ≈ 4890-4925 (tile t5). Where one crown ends and the next begins is a judgement.
- **groom at the horse's head** `S6.fig.groom_5290`: station −25.7, 5.6 m S; 1.4 m E of south-side house behind the willows (west of S5's house_5800); standing; faces W. Man with a round head and light clothes at the dark horse's shoulder on the near side, face turned west, perhaps holding the bridle; only head and shoulders show over the ridge. Nearest: man in a light robe riding the dark horse 1.3 m N, dark horse ridden behind the south-side roof 1.3 m NW, tall standing signboard behind the south-side ridge 1.7 m W. [est: foot; identification med; part: below the shoulders hidden by the ridge of the south-side roof] Note: Head u 5277-5300, v 1367-1392. Foot est from a standing height of ≈175 px; if he stoops, his feet are nearer the horse's (v ≈ 1480).
- **man in a light robe riding the dark horse** `S6.fig.rider_5335`: station −26.2, 4.4 m S; on the animal S6.ani.horse_5262; 1.2 m NE of south-side house behind the willows (west of S5's house_5800); riding; faces W. Man in a long light robe with his hair in a top-knot and band, sitting upright on the dark horse, face in profile to the west, both hands low at the reins. Nearest: dark horse ridden behind the south-side roof 0.4 m W, groom at the horse's head 1.3 m S, tall standing signboard behind the south-side ridge 2.3 m SW. [est: foot; identification med; part: below the seat hidden by the ridge] Note: Read as riding: his head is 40-70 px higher than the standing men behind the same ridge (S5.fig.roof_watcher_5712 head 1283, pole man 1317), a hatched saddle/stirrup shape lies under his robe (u 5340-5380, v 1372-1412), and his hands are at the horse's withers. Foot = ground under the seat (est). The brief's 'man at its head' is S6.fig.groom_5290.
- **dark horse ridden behind the south-side roof** `S6.ani.horse_5262`: station −26.5, 4.5 m S; 0.8 m NE of south-side house behind the willows (west of S5's house_5800); faces W; 1.05 m long. Dark horse with a dark mane and two short upright ears, its head lowered to the west, wearing a bridle; ridden by a man in a light robe, with a groom at its shoulder. Only the head, neck and back show above the ridge of the south-side roofs. Nearest: man in a light robe riding the dark horse 0.4 m E, groom at the horse's head 1.3 m SE, tall standing signboard behind the south-side ridge 2 m SW. [est: fore, hind; identification med; part: body and legs hidden by the ridge of the south-side roof (v ≈ 1430-1450)] Note: Ears (5203, 1355) and (5222, 1350); head down to the muzzle (5238, 1410); back v ≈ 1350-1360 toward the rider; a tail-like tuft at (5340-5370, 1373-1393) may be its tail. Species from the ears and long head: horse (could be a mule). Hooves est: the rider's head at 1240 puts the ground about 230 px lower, v ≈ 1470-1480 (same as the S5 rider ratio, head 1035 / foot 1246).
- **tall standing signboard behind the south-side ridge** `S6.prop.signboard_5129`: station −27.3, 6.4 m S; inside south-side house behind the willows (west of S5's house_5800). Tall narrow upright signboard with a pointed top and a double border, standing beyond the ridge of the south-side roof (on the street's south edge); its face carries three or four marks that may be characters, illegible: □□□. Nearest: head behind the ridge beside the signboard 0.3 m W, leaning poles behind the ridge 1.3 m W, groom at the horse's head 1.7 m E. [est: base; identification med; part: below v ≈ 1440 hidden by the ridge] Note: Board u 5114-5144, top point (5129, 1333), visible to the ridge at v 1440. Base est at the depth of the head beside it (standing-height estimate); if it stands nearer the ridge the base is higher (v ≈ 1480). Probably a shop sign of a south-side house facing the street. SCALE: board 30 px wide, ≥107 px visible.
- **head behind the ridge beside the signboard** `S6.fig.head_behind_ridge_5103`: station −27.6, 6.4 m S; inside south-side house behind the willows (west of S5's house_5800); standing; faces W. A man's head in profile, facing west, just above the ridge of the south-side roof beside the tall signboard; the rest is hidden. Nearest: tall standing signboard behind the south-side ridge 0.3 m E, leaning poles behind the ridge 1 m W, groom at the horse's head 2.1 m E. [est: foot; identification low; part: only the head shows above the ridge (v ≈ 1440)] Note: Head outline u 5092-5114, v 1396-1432. It touches the top of a leaning pole (5097, 1388), so it may instead be a rope loop hanging from the poles (the restyle paints a rope). Foot est from a standing height of ≈175 px.
- **leaning poles behind the ridge** `S6.prop.leaning_poles_5045`: station −28.6, 6.3 m S; inside south-side house behind the willows (west of S5's house_5800). Two or three long poles leaning together at a slant (tops to the north-east) just beyond the ridge of the south-side roof, next to an upright pole; a frame for an awning or poles stacked against a wall. Nearest: head behind the ridge beside the signboard 1 m E, tall standing signboard behind the south-side ridge 1.3 m E, dark horse ridden behind the south-side roof 2.7 m NE. [est: base; identification med; part: lower ends hidden by the ridge] Note: Poles (top -> where the ridge cuts them): (5057, 1365) -> (5025, 1440); (5097, 1388) -> (5057, 1440); a long one from (5097, 1390) to (5000, 1437). The upright pole at u 4993-5000 (top v 1343) is west of 5000 (tile t5). Base est at the depth of the signboard.
- **carter walking west beside the wheel** `S6.fig.walking_carter_5537`: station −28.7, 1.5 m N; 5.4 m N of south-side house behind the willows (west of S5's house_5800); walking; faces W. Man in a light short jacket and trousers with dark hair, walking west past the front of the cart's big wheel, back half turned to the viewer, arms at his sides; a carter of the sack cart. (sections.md S5/S6: second group of taxpayers) Nearest: big two-wheeled cart loaded with sacks 0.8 m N, bearded carter at the cart's front 2.6 m NW, dark team donkey (behind the near one) 3.6 m W. [part: shoulders overlapped by the sacks] Note: Front foot (5510, 1158), rear foot (5565, 1168). Head = top of the dark hair (u 5510-5540, v 984-1010).
- **big two-wheeled cart loaded with sacks** `S6.veh.sack_cart_5412`: station −29.1, 2.2 m N; 5.7 m SE of slatted railing in front of 孙羊正店 (east, by the 香□ box); faces W; 4.2 m long. Large two-wheeled cart piled high with big round roped sacks, drawn west by a donkey team on long slack traces and pushed from behind; one big spoked wheel shows on the near side. (sections.md S5/S6: second group of taxpayers (the cart S5.fig.cart_pusher_5727 pushes)) Nearest: carter walking west beside the wheel 0.8 m S, bearded carter at the cart's front 2.1 m W, pack donkey with its head lowered, by the depot 2.9 m NW. [est: front, rear; part: body hidden by the load and the carters; far wheel hidden] Note: Load (sacks) u ≈ 5407-5720, v 911-1100; the front sack begins at u ≈ 5407. Front = ground under the load's front where the traces attach (est); shafts are not clearly drawn. Rear est at the pusher's hands (S5.fig.cart_pusher_5727, foot 5727, 1155). Near wheel: hub (5608, 1048), rim bottom (5598, 1147), rim west edge (5520, 1100). A straight stick slants from the ground at (5440, 1133) up to (5480, 1062): a prop stick hanging under the cart's front or a carter's goad. A dark stick at (5640-5660, 1117-1167) under the rear is a prop stick or the pusher's (S5) gear. SCALE CUES: wheel diameter ≈ 200 px (radius 100 from hub to rim); load length ≈ 300 px; load top v ≈ 911. RECEDING: none drawn clearly; the cart is seen side-on.

### Stations −30.0 to −35.0

- **bare tree at the bottom edge, in front of the sedans** `S6.tree.bare_4665`: station −30.1, 10.6 m S; inside south-side houses east of the inn (roofs running back); about 5.36 m tall; crown 6.23 m wide. Large tree with no leaves drawn: fans of long, thin, straight twigs spreading up and out from forked branches (a bare or just-budding tree, perhaps a willow, but the twigs rise rather than hang), growing in front of the south-side houses and cut by the silk's bottom edge; its sprays hide the feet of the sedan bearers. Nearest: leaning poles behind the ridge 4.6 m NE, bare bush-tree at the bottom edge (west) 4.7 m E, head behind the ridge beside the signboard 4.9 m NE. [est: base; identification med; part: trunk cut by the silk's bottom edge] Note: Branch strokes converge on u 4650-4690 at the silk's bottom edge (v ~1770). Another such tree to the east (base ~5010) belongs to the next tile.
- **pack donkey with its head lowered, by the depot** `S6.ani.donkey_head_down_5565`: station −30.8, 4.5 m N; 3.7 m E of slatted railing in front of 孙羊正店 (east, by the 香□ box); faces SW; 0.54 m long. Grey-brown donkey standing between the wine depot and the sack cart's load, its rounded back up and its head lowered to the south-west; no load visible. The second pack donkey of the train that continues in S5. (sections.md S5: second group of taxpayers (pack donkeys and mules)) Nearest: dark-clad figure behind the bearded carter 1.5 m SW, bearded carter at the cart's front 2 m S, carter in a cap with folded arms 2.2 m W. [est: fore, hind; identification med; part: legs hidden by the cart's load; seen foreshortened] Note: Belongs with S5.grp.pack_train (S5.ani.donkey_pack_5660's note: 'a second donkey with its head lowered ... u≈5540-5620'). Body u 5540-5600, v 870-930; head u 5557-5583, v 920-953. Hooves est at the depth of S5's pack donkey's hooves (v ≈ 1000). The restyle paints a woman's face where its head is.
- **bearded carter at the cart's front** `S6.fig.bearded_carter_5428`: station −31.2, 2.5 m N; 3.8 m SE of slatted railing in front of 孙羊正店 (east, by the 香□ box); standing; faces SW. Bearded man in a short jacket with horizontal bands across his chest (a rope coil or a harness strap worn across the body), standing at the front of the sack cart between the team and the load, looking down to the south-west. (sections.md S5/S6: second group of taxpayers) Nearest: dark team donkey (behind the near one) 1 m W, team donkey with its head down (near side) 1.2 m W, dark-clad figure behind the bearded carter 1.4 m NW. [part: east side overlapped by the load's front sack] Note: Face u 5420-5460, v 957-990; banded chest v 1010-1057; legs u 5420-5447 down to the feet at v ≈ 1090-1105. - **banner 孫羊店** `S6.prop.banner_sunyangdian_4152`: station −50.1, 4.8 m N; 0.5 m S of 孙羊店 butcher's shop. Long vertical cloth banner with a dark border reading 孫羊店 top to bottom, hanging from the slanting eave beam on the east side of the 孫羊店 building, between a pale cloth panel to its west and another to its east; characters clear. (sections.md / brief: the 孙羊店 butcher's flag) Nearest: young man beside the high seat 0.5 m W, man on a high seat behind the tray stall 1.3 m W, tall chair of the stall keeper 1.6 m W. [est: base; identification med] Note: Banner u 4135-4170, top at the eave (v ~585), bottom v ~740, characters at v 610-690. It hangs from the building's eave, not from a pole of its own: if the structures agent lists it in that building's what, drop this entity. Base = ground below it, est at the building's front line (its post at u 4202-4236 reaches v ~925). MERGE: the structures pass reads the flag as hung from a pole of its own, running straight from (4085,545) at the butcher's roof's west end down to (4445,835) at the 正店's west column, where its foot probably stands; the pole is part of this entity. (名画记: 方井 (pin 47, ≈2110); 余辉: 深井汲水) Nearest: small box or pail at the foot of the pole 0.7 m NW, man hauling a bucket up at the well's east end 0.8 m S, man drawing water with a rope 1.8 m W. - **man hauling a bucket up at the well's east end** `S6.fig.well_bucket_hauler_2196`: station −74.2, 4.6 m N; 0.2 m E of square well (方井) with its 田-shaped cover; standing; faces W. Man in a dark cap and a light jacket at the east end of the well, bent double toward the well mouth: his striped, rolled left sleeve reaches down to the shaft and his right hand grips the knotted rope handle of a wooden bucket he is lifting over the cover's east beam. (名画记: 方井 (pin 47, ≈2110); 余辉: 深井汲水) Nearest: tall pole at the east end of the well 0.8 m N, small box or pail at the foot of the pole 1.3 m N, man drawing water with a rope 1.6 m W. Painted height ~160 px (bent double).

### Stations −75.0 to −80.0

- **man drawing water with a rope** `S6.fig.well_rope_drawer_2097`: station −75.8, 4.9 m N; H 0.25 m (well cover: S6.str.square_well_2000 (its back beam)); inside square well (方井) with its 田-shaped cover; standing; faces S. Man in a dark cap and a short belted jacket with rolled sleeves, standing on the back beam of the well's cover facing us, bent forward with both hands on the well rope that hangs straight down into the shaft: he is drawing water. (名画记: 方井 (pin 47, ≈2110); 余辉: 深井汲水) Nearest: man heaving a bucket onto his shoulder at the well 1 m W, small box or pail at the foot of the pole 1.5 m NE, man hauling a bucket up at the well's east end 1.6 m E. Note: rope at u 2068, v 815-911; feet at u 2080-2111, v 895-911. Painted height ~155 px (bent).
- **man heaving a bucket onto his shoulder at the well** `S6.fig.well_bucket_lifter_2032`: station −76.7, 5.1 m N; H 0.25 m (well cover: S6.str.square_well_2000 (its back beam)); inside square well (方井) with its 田-shaped cover; carrying; faces N. Water-carrier in a short belted jacket and leggings standing on the back beam of the well's cover, half turned away from us, heaving a full round wooden bucket up onto his left shoulder; his head is tucked beside (and half hidden by) the bucket. (名画记: 方井 (pin 47, ≈2110); 余辉: 深井汲水) Nearest: man drawing water with a rope 1 m E, small box or pail at the foot of the pole 2.3 m E, man hauling a bucket up at the well's east end 2.5 m E. Painted height ~145 px, bent under the load.
- **figure cut by the silk's top edge** `S6.fig.top_edge_lower_body_2775`: station −78.6, 22.0 m N; inside building beyond the compound (top edge); standing; faces S. Faded dark shape above the far roof ridge at the silk's top edge that reads as the lower body of a person in a dark robe, the rest cut off by the edge. Nearest: needle-leaved tree by the gable 5.8 m SW, man at the west end of a lattice frame 6.2 m SE, long low lattice frame on the back street 6.3 m E. [est: foot; identification low; faded] Note: strokes at u 2752-2795, v 0-45: two leg-like dark strokes ending at v 40-45 above the ridge (v ~50-56); two thin vertical strokes beside it at u 2707 and 2720 are unidentified (possibly part of a building beyond the ridge). Could also be foliage; the restyle paints draped figures. Head is off the silk.

## Groups

- **drawing water at the square well** `S6.grp.square_well_drawers` (stations −74.1 to −76.7): Three men draw water at the square well with its 田-shaped wooden cover: on its back beam one heaves a full bucket onto his shoulder while the next, facing us, draws on the rope that hangs into the shaft; at the east end a third, bent double, lifts a bucket out by its rope. A tall pole with a small box at its foot stands at the platform's east end. Members: man heaving a bucket onto his shoulder at the well (−76.7, 5.1 m N); man drawing water with a rope (−75.8, 4.9 m N); man hauling a bucket up at the well's east end (−74.2, 4.6 m N); tall pole at the east end of the well (−74.1, 5.4 m N); small box or pail at the foot of the pole (−74.6, 5.9 m N).
- **men talking in front of the paper stall** `S6.grp.stall_talkers` (stations −68.8 to −71.2): In front of the paper stall's west end three men in long robes stand together talking (one facing us, one with his back to us, one tall and dark-robed facing west); beside them a man in a tall black cap looks up at them with a hand at his chin, and a boy in a short jacket stands by him. Members: boy (child) in a short jacket (−71.2, 0.9 m N); man in a tall black cap (−69.9, on the centre line); man in a long grey robe by the stall (−70.1, 1.2 m N); robed man with his back to us (−68.8, 0.7 m N); tall man in a dark robe at the stall (−69.0, 1.7 m N).
- **the paper-horse stall** `S6.grp.paper_stall` (stations −68.2 to −70.9): Under three big dark umbrellas a long table sells paper offerings for burning (paper figures, a tiered paper tower, a tray of small paper goods); the keeper stands behind its east end. A customer with a dark bundle stands at its east end (t2). Members: paper-horse stall table (−69.4, 3.4 m N); big umbrella over the stall's west half (−70.8, 2.9 m N); big umbrella behind the stall (−70.2, 3.8 m N); big umbrella over the stall's east end (−68.2, 3.5 m N); paper offering figure (west) (−70.9, 4.0 m N); tiered paper tower (−70.5, 3.9 m N); paper offering figure with arms out (−69.9, 3.9 m N); paper figure with a hat (−69.5, 3.9 m N); tray of small paper goods (−68.6, 3.2 m N); paper-stall keeper (−68.5, 3.4 m N).
- **the ring round the 解 sign** `S6.grp.jie_ring` (stations −64.1 to −68.4): Under a flat mat awning beside the tall 解 signboard, young men sit and stand in a ring round a pale-robed man seated on a bench with his back to us: a hooded old man and two smiling young men face him from the back, listeners sit at his west and east, men stand behind and beside them, a child stands at the bench's end, and a man with folded arms watches from the north-east. Members: the 解 signboard (−65.7, 10.7 m S); flat mat awning on posts (−67.3, 6.3 m S); bench in the ring (−66.7, 8.1 m S); small dark box in the ring (−68.4, 6.1 m S); pale-robed man seated at the ring's centre (−66.9, 7.8 m S); hooded old man at the back of the ring (−67.6, 6.0 m S); young man seated facing us in the ring (−67.9, 6.4 m S); smiling young man with knees up (−67.1, 6.5 m S); listener at the ring's west side (−68.0, 7.0 m S); young man in a pale cap at the ring's east side (−66.1, 7.6 m S); man in a dark robe with his back to us by the post (−65.2, 8.5 m S); man in tan seated at the awning's east edge (−65.7, 6.5 m S); faded man in a pale robe at the ring's back (−65.7, 7.1 m S); young man standing at the ring's south side (−65.4, 9.8 m S); small child at the bench's east end (−66.0, 8.3 m S); man with folded arms behind the rider (−64.1, 8.4 m S).

- **the storyteller and his listeners (闹市说书)** `S6.grp.storyteller_ring` (stations −50.9 to −55.1): A crowd stands in a ring on the street in front of the 孫羊店 meat counter listening to a bearded storyteller who faces out of the ring toward the viewer: most listeners turn their backs to us, two men stand beside him facing into the ring, a child stands at its front edge. Nine members are east of u 3800 (listed here); about ten more stand west of u 3800 (tile t3), about 19 people in all. Members: storyteller (闹市说书) (−53.1, 4.1 m N); listener standing beside the storyteller (−52.3, 4.3 m N); listener in a dark hat beside the storyteller (−51.9, 4.2 m N); listener in a pale jacket (front row) (−51.9, 2.6 m N); listener in the second row (−51.8, 3.0 m N); listener in a long dark robe (−51.0, 2.6 m N); listener in a dark purple robe (east edge) (−51.0, 3.2 m N); listener in a pale jacket with a bag at his hip (−50.9, 1.8 m N); child listener (−52.3, 2.5 m N); tall bearded man in a brown robe (ring, west end) (−55.1, 3.4 m N); listener with a knotted cap and a dark fan (−54.7, 4.0 m N); listener bending forward in a light robe (−54.2, 2.7 m N); listener in a long dark robe (−54.2, 3.2 m N); listener with a dark hair bun (−54.1, 3.6 m N); man standing behind the crowd by the corner building (−54.8, 5.2 m N); big listener bending forward in a light robe and dark hat (−53.2, 2.6 m N); listener at the ring's east side (head only) (−53.3, 3.7 m N); half-hidden listener in the ring (face only) (−54.1, 3.9 m N).
- **the tray stall with the high seat** `S6.grp.tray_stall_4110` (stations −48.1 to −52.2): A street stall in front of the 孫羊店 building, east of the storyteller's ring: an oval tray of goods on a crossed-leg stand with a basket under it; a man on a high chair sits behind it with a young man beside him, a man bends over the tray from its east end, and a man holding a small jar stands at its west end; a slanted dotted board on a frame stands behind its west end. Members: man on a high seat behind the tray stall (−50.9, 5.2 m N); tall chair of the stall keeper (−51.0, 5.3 m N); young man beside the high seat (−50.7, 4.8 m N); slanted dotted board on a frame (−52.2, 6.2 m N); oval tray of goods on a crossed-leg stand (−49.2, 3.0 m N); goods on the oval tray (−49.3, 3.0 m N); round basket under the tray (−49.9, 3.0 m N); man bending over the tray (−48.1, 3.0 m N); man holding a small red jar (−50.6, 3.0 m N).
- **the parasol stall** `S6.grp.parasol_stall_4300` (stations −45.4 to −47.1): A stall under a big tilted parasol at the west end of 孫羊正店's street front: two oval trays of goods on folding stands with a tub of upright sticks between them and a net bag hanging under the parasol. No seller is drawn at it; the seated man at the 正店's door (east of u 4400, tile t5) may keep it. Members: big parasol over the stall (−46.1, 2.5 m N); net bag hanging under the parasol (−46.5, 2.5 m N); oval tray on a folding stand (west of the tub) (−47.0, 2.8 m N); goods on the west tray (−47.1, 2.8 m N); wooden tub between the trays (−46.0, 2.5 m N); bundle of upright sticks in the tub (−46.0, 2.5 m N); oval tray on a folding stand (east of the tub) (−45.4, 2.7 m N); goods on the east tray (−45.4, 2.8 m N).
- **a seated man and a crouching man** `S6.grp.seated_pair_3900` (stations −47.9 to −48.1): On the south side of the street west of the fan man, a man in a square black cap sits facing the viewer while a man in a pale jacket crouches in front of him, bent over something in his hands. Members: man in a square black cap, seated (−48.1, 1.8 m S); man crouching in front of the seated man (−47.9, 2.3 m S).
- **two men under a dark umbrella with a stack of boxes** `S6.grp.umbrella_pair_4060` (stations −43.8 to −45.1): On the south side of the street, a youth and a round-faced man stand under one wide dark umbrella with a pole between them at shoulder height and a stack of flat boxes below it; a man looks up beside the boxes. They may be carrying the boxes on the pole. Members: youth under a dark umbrella (−45.0, 3.5 m S); round-faced man under the umbrella's east edge (−43.9, 3.9 m S); stack of flat boxes (−45.1, 2.6 m S); man looking up beside the box stack (−43.8, 3.7 m S).
- **the scholar studying upstairs** `S6.grp.scholar_studying` (stations −35.9 to −37.1): In the upper storey of the inn 久住王員外家, a young scholar sits at the window with his hand to his chin beside a table with a lamp and books (士子苦读). ## Open questions

- **Section:** Things far up the silk are painted smaller: the top street's adults are 100-125 px tall against 175-243 px on the main street, the cloth shop's posts 88 px against ~210 px for the corner shop's, and the wheelbarrow at the top has a 52 px wheel against 76-85 px for the barrows in the street (about 0.55-0.6 at D ~25-30 m). The frame keeps one across-street scale (77 px/m), so buildings, bays and gaps at the back of the silk come out about 0.6 of their painted proportion. A depth-dependent scale would change S5's frame too: the lead's call.
- **Section:** Roof rises are painted shorter than the frame allows (a 2 m rise is 154 px of v; the compound's north range shows 143 px for its whole south slope, the 正店's upper roof 205 px): heights read from roofs are unreliable.
- **Section:** The street's south edge is taken at v 1440 (the narrowest reading; S5's value). Figures seen over the south-side ridge east of u 5000 (the rider, his groom, the pole man) imply ground at v ~1480-1575, and S5's house_5800 has porch posts with feet at v 1694-1758: the houses' street fronts may lie up to ~150 px lower.
- **Section:** The 正店's upper floor height (3.4-6.0 m, 4.5 assumed) and the inn's (3.4 assumed) are not measurable: the ground under the 正店's balustrade is hidden and the inn's is beyond the silk.
- **Section:** The receding edges at the east end (u 5272-5455) read dx ~51 where the frame, blending to S5's 58.24 at the seam, gives ~57.6. S5's 58.24 west of the gate was carried from the gate, not measured; if the lead lowers S5's west value toward ~52, this section's last knot follows it.
- **Section:** The cross street runs due north (both its sides recede at the section's direction); no continuation south of the main street is painted: south of it lies an open square between the 解 building (S7) and the inn's west wing.
- **Section:** S6.veh.hidden_wagon_3400: only the second wagon's four-mule team, driver and traces are painted; its body is hidden behind the incense shop's lean-to mat roof. Its position is inferred.
- `S6.fig.crouching_dark_5515` (crouching dark-clad figure behind the ridge): Dark crumpled shape u 5487-5545, v 1347-1400 with an arm-like curve at (5487-5517, 1357-1370); no face is drawn. Its height (≈130 px above the est ground) suits a crouching man better than a sack, and the restyle also paints a crouching man, but it may be a dark bundle.
- `S6.fig.upper_standing_5452` (man standing at the table, upper storey (east part)): UPPER FLOOR (east room, u 5410-5640): balustrade top rail v ≈ 400-405, plank panel v 405-443, floor line v ≈ 445. Foot est on that line. Painted height 122, smaller than street adults (~170-180); he may be seated on a high bench (not painted).
- `S6.fig.dark_figure_5440` (dark-clad figure behind the bearded carter): No face is drawn. The dark shaggy shape with a light curved line below it (u 5442-5474, v 904-924) could also be the raised neck of a dark animal with a collar; the restyle paints a man in a dark robe here. Foot est from a standing height of ≈175 px.
- `S6.fig.rider_5335` (man in a light robe riding the dark horse): Read as riding: his head is 40-70 px higher than the standing men behind the same ridge (S5.fig.roof_watcher_5712 head 1283, pole man 1317), a hatched saddle/stirrup shape lies under his robe (u 5340-5380, v 1372-1412), and his hands are at the horse's withers. Foot = ground under the seat (est). The brief's 'man at its head' is S6.fig.groom_5290.
- `S6.fig.groom_5290` (groom at the horse's head): Head u 5277-5300, v 1367-1392. Foot est from a standing height of ≈175 px; if he stoops, his feet are nearer the horse's (v ≈ 1480).
- `S6.fig.drinker_upper_5140` (drinker in the 正店's upper storey (west part)): UPPER FLOOR: floor line (beam under the balustrade) at v ≈ 438 under u 5000-5250; balustrade v 395-435. Foot est on that floor line under the head; seated painted height ≈ 100. West of him (u 5063-5123, v 360-403) is a big concentric swirl that matches the scaffold's flower/cloud ornaments lower down (u 5150-5210, v 520-600), so it is not recorded as a figure or prop; the restyle paints figures there and at u 5000-5060, but the scroll's strokes there are the scaffold's flower bunches.
- `S6.fig.head_behind_ridge_5103` (head behind the ridge beside the signboard): Head outline u 5092-5114, v 1396-1432. It touches the top of a leaning pole (5097, 1388), so it may instead be a rope loop hanging from the poles (the restyle paints a rope). Foot est from a standing height of ≈175 px.
- `S6.fig.capped_man_5090` (man in a dark cap at the door, holding a pole): Face u 5077-5097, v 880-903. The slanting double line (pole) runs from about (5007, 897) to (5127, 937); read with the restyle as a pole he holds (med). Foot est from a standing height of ≈165 px.
- `S6.fig.donkey_leader_5088` (man in white leading the dark donkey): Feet (5075, 1107) and (5105, 1093). A line runs from his hands (≈5075, 1000) to the donkey's muzzle (≈5125, 1010); read as a lead rope (med). The big round wicker basket west of him (u 4910-5065, v 980-1120, base ≈ 4985, 1120) is tile t5's.
- `S6.fig.doorman_5015` (man in a dark cap in the 正店's doorway): Robe visible down to v ≈ 900; foot est at the shop-front line (the 栀子灯's stand stands on v ≈ 900). Painted height ≈ 140, a little less than the street figures, as the door figures generally are.
- `S6.fig.man_child_on_shoulders_4975` (man carrying a child on his shoulders): Face u 4960-4987, v 855-873.
- `S6.fig.child_on_shoulders_4977` (child on a man's shoulders): Like a rider: foot = the ground under the seat (the man's foot point). Head u 4970-4985, v 817-837; about 20 px of child shows above the man's cap.
- `S6.fig.youth_hair_bun_stick_4950` (young person with a hair bun and a long stick): Stick at u 4939-4947, v 943-987. Feet hidden by the basket.
- `S6.fig.man_behind_stooping_4925` (man standing behind the stooping man): Head u 4909-4929, v 918-934. Feet estimated from a ~172 px painted height.
- `S6.fig.shop_interior_faint_4905` (faint figure in the back of the shop front): Silhouette u 4882-4930, v 750-840, legs v 810-840. The restyle shows a standing man here.

- `S6.fig.ridge_dark_robe_3575` (figure in a dark robe north of the inn's roof): u 3557-3587, v 1310-1413; the restyle paints a woman in red here. Strokes faint.
- `S6.fig.ridge_reclining_3530` (man reclining beside a dark heap north of the inn's roof): dark mass u 3470-3560, v 1320-1400 with a pale face (3530-3553, 1343-1367). It could instead be an animal's head (a donkey lying) or a bundle; the restyle paints a crouching man with a bundle.
- `S6.fig.bowing_dark_robe_3470` (man in a dark robe bowing with joined hands): feet (3448-3502, 1268-1272). Could be a woman (the knot is a bun); dress reads male.
- `S6.fig.groom_whip_3452` (groom with a long whip walking by the horse): feet (3447, 912) and (3462, 897, lifted); whip from (3505, 810) curving down to (3500, 910).
- `S6.fig.box_shoulder_porter_3405` (man carrying a long flat box on his shoulder): box u 3319-3428, v 1050-1075. His tie to the scholars is a reading, not painted.

- `S6.fig.top_edge_figure_3332` (figure cut by the silk's top edge behind the barrow): the restyle paints a standing man in a red robe here. Could also be a tall load standing on the barrow; strokes faint.
- `S6.fig.box_carrier_3272` (man carrying a stack of boxes on his back): feet: (3253, 105) and (3290, 110). Box stack u 3218-3256, v 22-67.
- `S6.fig.lattice_frame_east_3180` (man leaning on the east end of a lattice frame): far figure; lower body seen to v ~157 then hidden by the roof slope.
- `S6.fig.lattice_frame_west_3092` (man at the west end of a lattice frame): far figure: head-to-waist about 50 px; foot estimated from the porter on the same street at u ~3250 (about 105 px tall).
- `S6.fig.tray_vendor_3050` (vendor carrying a long tray): tray u 3023-3127, v 787-817. Foot estimated from a 175 px height.
- `S6.fig.man_behind_tree_3040` (man half hidden behind the tree): probably seated at the low table; feet estimated at the silk's edge.
- `S6.fig.small_child_3035` (small child in dark clothes): CHILD: about 80 px tall (head estimated) against ~175 for the men; could instead be a dark bag held low. The restyle paints a small child.
- `S6.fig.seated_by_pole_2970` (man seated by the umbrella pole): seated on a stool or bench not clearly painted; feet estimated.
- `S6.fig.stall_man_2890` (man behind the crowd under the east umbrella): foot estimated from a 175 px height; the restyle paints a brown robe.
- `S6.fig.child_by_dark_robe_2880` (child at the tall man's side): CHILD: painted height 133 against ~178 for the men beside him. Read from a dark head at (2876,913) and a light leg ending (2880,1046); the restyle paints a child there.
- `S6.fig.grey_back_2862` (man with his back to us at the stall): only a head and shoulder outline in faded strokes; the restyle paints a grey-clad man here.
- `S6.fig.white_jacket_skirt_2795` (man in a white jacket and dark skirt, back to us): feet estimated under the skirt (skirt u 2780-2815, hem v ~997).

- `S6.fig.bearded_light_robe_2730` (bearded man in a light robe): one foot visible at (2733,1041); the other hidden. Painted height 188.
- `S6.fig.light_robe_behind_2650` (man in a light robe behind the round-faced man): foot estimated about 170 px below the head, behind the round-faced man.
- `S6.fig.porter_topknot_2605` (porter with a top-knot and a tall cylindrical basket): Pole at v ~1170-1180 from u ~2510 to ~2690; cylindrical basket u 2515-2549, v 1227-1279. Feet hidden; est ~190 px below the head. Recorded in the merge (t1 saw him; t2 took him for the front porter).
- `S6.fig.paper_stall_seller_2565` (paper-stall keeper): head just under the east umbrella's rim; foot from a standing height of ~175 px.

- `S6.fig.donkey_groom_pale_2200` (man in a pale robe holding the donkey's head): hem u 2177-2222 at v 1693-1704; a dark shoe at (2200, 1715). Painted height 190 px.
