- `S6.fig.jie_pale_cap_2175` (young man in a pale cap at the ring's east side): identification med
- `S6.fig.leaning_over_awning_2168` (man leaning over the awning's back edge): hands at (2130, 1250) and (2215, 1252); foot from a standing height of ~185 px. He may be resting on a hidden rail.
- `S6.fig.jie_child_2143` (small child at the bench's east end): painted height 75 px against 182 px for the young man beside him; could instead be a crouching adult.
- `S6.fig.jie_old_man_2138` (hooded old man at the back of the ring): seat and feet hidden; foot from a seated height of ~125 px. Either he or the pale-robed man seated with his back to us is the fortune-teller.
- `S6.fig.jie_seated_back_2105` (pale-robed man seated at the ring's centre): seated on S6.prop.bench_2100 (robe hem on its front edge, v ~1572).
- `S6.fig.jie_listener_west_2062` (listener at the ring's west side): identification med
- `S6.fig.well_bucket_lifter_2032` (man heaving a bucket onto his shoulder at the well): bucket on his shoulder at u 2003-2042, v 753-780 (carried); foot at u 2026-2038, v 892-899, on the cover's back beam (the back rail runs at v 868-880). Painted height ~145 px, bent under the load.
- `S6.fig.crouching_dark_2028` (man in dark crouching behind the awning): body ends at the awning's back edge (v ~1265). If he is standing instead, his feet are near (2028, 1370).
- `S6.ani.donkey_head_down_5565` (pack donkey with its head lowered, by the depot): Belongs with S5.grp.pack_train (S5.ani.donkey_pack_5660's note: 'a second donkey with its head lowered ... u≈5540-5620'). - `S6.ani.donkey_team_dark_5300` (dark team donkey (behind the near one)): Dark back v 984-999 from u 5299 to 5410 (rump at u 5387-5410); hind hoof (5405, 1095). The dark head with the ring (u 5247-5272, v 905-945) is read as this donkey's, raised; it could instead be the pale donkey's head. Fore est under its withers, behind the near donkey's body.
- `S6.ani.donkey_team_pale_5285` (pale team donkey (far side)): Body outline u 5266-5360, v 920-983, tassel at (5335, 955-980). The low shop-front railing (v 887-914) runs just above it. Hooves est north of the dark donkey's (v ≈ 1072); head est at the body's west end.
- `S6.ani.horse_5262` (dark horse ridden behind the south-side roof): Ears (5203, 1355) and (5222, 1350); head down to the muzzle (5238, 1410); back v ≈ 1350-1360 toward the rider; a tail-like tuft at (5340-5370, 1373-1393) may be its tail. Species from the ears and long head: horse (could be a mule). Hooves est: the rider's head at 1240 puts the ground about 230 px lower, v ≈ 1470-1480 (same as the S5 rider ratio, head 1035 / foot 1246).
- `S6.ani.donkey_door_5150` (dark donkey at the 正店 door): Body u 5110-5247, v 922-993 (haunch u 5215-5247); head lowered, poll ≈ (5135, 960), pale muzzle u 5120-5143, v 990-1033. Team membership is a reading: no trace from it to the cart is clear (low).
- `S6.ani.dog_4335` (small dark animal under the south-side parasol): Body u 4302-4367, v 1345-1375. Lines above it (u 4305-4375, v 1327-1345) may instead be a person bending; the restyle paints a man here.
- `S6.ani.mule_upper_3_3655` (third mule of the upper team): the upper team's heads at u 3562-3670 are hard to separate: four backs are painted, and four or five head shapes; recorded as four mules.
- `S6.ani.mule_upper_2_3620` (second mule of the upper team): identification med
- `S6.ani.mule_upper_1_3585` (rear mule of the upper team): hind hooves (3500, 362) and (3525, 348, lifted); front hooves (3578, 362) and (3600, 352).
- `S6.ani.mule_lower_3_3498` (third mule of the big wagon's team): eye at (3501, 614); hoof (3497, 680); lifted hooves (3517, 657).
- `S6.ani.mule_lower_2_3462` (second mule of the big wagon's team (front right)): hoof (3462, 680); lifted hoof (3475, 660).
- `S6.ani.horse_3445` (rider's dark horse, seen from behind): hind hooves (3330, 1028) and (3395, 1025); one front hoof (3457, 1002). - `S6.ani.donkey_wine_cart_3105` (dark donkey of the wine cart): hind hooves at u 3000-3050, v 1477-1490 just above the umbrella's rim. Which donkey is in the shafts is unclear; the shaft line runs to this one's shoulder.
- `S6.ani.donkey_2285` (dark donkey with a rider): hooves: near hind (2333, 1727), far hind (2316, 1699), near fore lifted (2291, 1706); body u 2233-2356, back at v ~1575; a dark tail hangs at the rump (u 2338-2368). SCALE CUE: hoof to back ~135 px.
- `S6.veh.hidden_wagon_3400` (second wagon, hidden behind the corner shed): Position inferred only from the traces, which vanish behind the roof's tip at about (3350-3420, 285-305). Rear end and size are guesses (a wagon as long as the big one); it may stand further north behind the shed. A builder may leave the body out or hide it behind the shed.
- `S6.veh.barrow_casks_3300` (loaded 串车 barrow at the incense shop's corner): Wheel nearly round (its plane faces the viewer, so the barrow runs east-west): rim u 3177-3253, v 608-684, diameter about 76 px, hub (3215, 645). Load u 3140-3300, v 535-640; handles at (3150-3165, 630-645). front = ground under the raised east rack, on the wheel's ground line (est). SEAM: its pusher (foot ≈ (3128, 716)) belongs to t2; this barrow is mine by its front (east) end, which is an inference from the pusher at the west end. t2 may record it too. MERGE: t2 recorded the same cart as S6.veh.handcart_3100 (front at the west shaft tips (3100,712), body with lattice sides heaped with dark sacks and bundles, a hook hanging at its north-east end, wheel 80 x 87 px, hub (3215,645)); retired as a duplicate. Whether it has one wheel (串车) or two (only the near one shows) is open.
- `S6.veh.barrow_top_3340` (wheelbarrow at the top of the silk): SCALE CUES: wheel rim u 3318-3360, v 80-132, hub about (3340, 104): diameter about 52 px. Bed from the front tip (3271, 63) to the handle ends (≈3411, 72); rear legs at (3385-3400, 94-120). front/rear = ground under the bed's ends, on the wheel's ground line (est).
- `S6.veh.wine_cart_3090` (donkey cart with two wine casks): front = ground under the shaft tips at the donkeys' shoulders (shaft seen from (2975,1475) to (3088,1408)); rear = ground under the rear end (rear rail u ~2700). Casks: near (south) cask top ellipse u 2705-2840, v 1480-1520, lid knob (2770,1498); far cask u 2770-2900, v 1440-1463, knob (2837,1452); basket u 2707-2790, v 1520-1597. Under the rear a curved frame and a post reach the ground at (2748,1685) (a rear prop or a small rear wheel, unclear). SCALE: big wheel rim u 2845-2935, v 1460-1690, hub (2890,1547): 230 px tall; cask lid 135 px across; bed about 240 px long (u 2700-2940).
- `S6.tree.bare_shoots_5105` (bare bush-tree at the bottom edge (east)): Shoots converge toward u ≈ 5087-5123 at the silk edge; the eastmost shoots cross the roof tiles at u 5150-5240.
- `S6.tree.bare_shoots_5012` (bare bush-tree at the bottom edge (west)): Shoots converge toward u ≈ 4994-5050 at the silk edge. The twig mass u 4880-5250 reads as two or three such trees: this one, S6.tree.bare_shoots_5105, and one converging at u ≈ 4890-4925 (tile t5). Where one crown ends and the next begins is a judgement.
- `S6.tree.bare_4665` (bare tree at the bottom edge, in front of the sedans): Branch strokes converge on u 4650-4690 at the silk's bottom edge (v ~1770). Another such tree to the east (base ~5010) belongs to the next tile.
- `S6.tree.far_tree_top_4555` (far tree top above the roof): Fans at u 4497-4613, v 0-42, separated from the pine_4430 crown by clean roof tiles. Its base is hidden behind the roof (possibly above the silk's top edge); set just below the visible crown top. Place it as a far background tree behind that roof.
- `S6.tree.pine_4430` (pine-like tree west of the 正店's upper storey): Trunk u 4422-4438 visible from v ~130 to ~392. Base estimated just behind the roof that hides it (ground ~15 m north of the street). Species read from the needle fans; could be another conifer.
- `S6.tree.willow_crown_4230` (tree crown at the top edge behind the roofs): Strands at u 4140-4320, v 0-60, cut by the silk's top edge. It may be the upper west part of the big willow whose trunk shows at u ~4420-4445, v 20-370 (base east of u 4400: tile t5); if t5 records that crown reaching here, drop this. Base est behind the roofs at about the top street's depth.
- `S6.tree.bare_lower_3527` (bare tree at the lower edge (east)): twigs converge near (3507-3547, 1713-1740).
- `S6.tree.bare_lower_3400` (bare tree at the lower edge (middle)): twigs converge near (3387-3413, 1713-1740).
- `S6.tree.bare_lower_3228` (bare tree at the lower edge (west)): twigs converge near (3213-3240, 1673-1693); base est at the silk's edge (v ≈ 1768). The crowns of the three lower-edge trees overlap; the count is read from where the twigs converge.
- `S6.tree.needle_tree_2245` (needle-leaved tree by the gable): foliage u 2200-2290, v 45-170, over roof lines; the needle strokes run on without a break from the tree at u 2160, so this may be that tree's east half.
- `S6.tree.needle_tree_2160` (needle-leaved tree in the courtyard behind (east)): foliage u 2129-2195, v 31-260.
- `S6.tree.needle_tree_2085` (small needle-leaved tree in the courtyard): foliage u 2047-2118, v 37-130; may be the outer part of the crowns beside it.
- `S6.tree.needle_tree_2025` (needle-leaved tree in the courtyard behind (west)): foliage u 1985-2055, v 12-250; no trunk drawn. The crowns of this grove overlap; separate crowns read by their tops.
- `S6.prop.ewer_upper_5507` (wine ewer on the upper-room table): Drawn at u 5500-5515, v 355-382.
- `S6.prop.table_upper_5497` (table in the upper room (east part)): Top spans u ≈ 5462-5530. Base est on the floor line (v ≈ 445) under the top's middle.
- `S6.prop.dishes_upper_5475` (dishes on the upper-room table): Small shapes at u 5462-5490, v 375-390; one entity for the set.
- `S6.prop.gardenia_lamp_5327` (栀子灯 (gourd-shaped lamp) east of the 正店 door): Neck from v ≈ 780, belly u 5313-5340, v 830-873. Pairs with S6.prop.gardenia_lamp_5055 across the door. The restyle repaints it as a man in a red robe; the scroll shows only the gourd outline.
- `S6.prop.counter_inside_5238` (counter seen through the 正店 door): Front face u 5200-5275, v 840-860; objects on top at v 805-840 (u 5203-5225 and 5235-5251). - `S6.prop.lampbox_xiang_5160` (lamp-box marked 香 at the 正店 door): Box face u 5135-5183, v 803-880 (香 at v 813-853; the second character's top strokes at v 860-877, probably 醪 per sections.md, not legible here). Cap v 783-800, flower spray above to v ≈ 695. Posts at u 5127-5137 and 5183-5193 frame it; they may be the box's own stand or the 彩楼欢门's posts (if the box hangs on the 欢门, it belongs to that structure: structures agent please check). Base est at the shop front, below the hidden stand. SCALE: box face 48 × 77 px.
- `S6.prop.signboard_5129` (tall standing signboard behind the south-side ridge): Board u 5114-5144, top point (5129, 1333), visible to the ridge at v 1440. Base est at the depth of the head beside it (standing-height estimate); if it stands nearer the ridge the base is higher (v ≈ 1480). Probably a shop sign of a south-side house facing the street. SCALE: board 30 px wide, ≥107 px visible.
- `S6.prop.vessel_upper_5060` (small vessel on a stand (upper storey, west)): Drawn at u 5053-5070, v 313-343, standing on a short horizontal bar (shelf or table top) at v ≈ 341, i.e. about 100 px above the floor line (v ≈ 438); the table or shelf itself is not drawn below it. Could also be a finial of the 彩楼 scaffold seen through the room (low conf).
- `S6.prop.leaning_poles_5045` (leaning poles behind the ridge): Poles (top -> where the ridge cuts them): (5057, 1365) -> (5025, 1440); (5097, 1388) -> (5057, 1440); a long one from (5097, 1390) to (5000, 1437). - `S6.prop.pole_capped_4997` (upright capped pole at the street's south edge (east)): u 4994-5000, v 1347-1437. At the boundary: base u ~4997.
- `S6.prop.tripod_stand_knob_4942` (tall tripod stand with a knob (east)): Knob/apex (4942, 1337-1353); feet at u 4925, 4943, 4958 at v ~1443.
- `S6.prop.tripod_stand_4880` (tall tripod stand at the street's south edge (middle)): Apex (4877, 1337); feet at u 4860, 4880, 4907 at v ~1440-1447; cross-bar at v ~1390.
- `S6.prop.tripod_stand_4822` (tall tripod stand at the street's south edge (west)): Apex (4820, 1347); feet at u 4805, 4822, 4840 on the beam at v ~1437-1442. 93 px tall. The beam runs u 4797-4963 at v 1433-1443; it may be a roof ridge (then the stands would be on the roof).
- `S6.prop.pole_4770` (upright pole at the street's south edge (west)): u 4767-4773, v 1340-1437.
- `S6.prop.lampbox_sun_4665` (lamp-box reading 孫□): Cap u 4648-4680, v 782-797; panel u 4653-4677, v 797-870. The column beside it is at u 4630-4650. If it hangs on the column it belongs to the building.
- `S6.prop.stool_4461` (small stool under the seated man): Seat top ~v 990-1000; leg u 4456-4466 down to v 1044.
- `S6.prop.gardenia_lamp_4422` (栀子灯 (gourd-shaped lamp) at the 正店's west door): Lamp u 4400-4445, v 760-870 (outline of neck and belly; the restyle paints it as a red gourd too). - `S6.prop.sticks_in_tub_4325` (bundle of upright sticks in the tub): Sticks u 4305-4355, v 875-982.
- `S6.prop.parasol_4312` (big parasol over the stall): Canopy u 4162-4399, v 725-845. SCALE: canopy 237 px across. The pole is not clearly drawn between the hub and the net bag; the straight line at u 4299-4305, v 845-980 and the restyle suggest it stands in the tub.
- `S6.prop.net_bag_4286` (net bag hanging under the parasol): Bag u 4272-4299, v 845-885; base = ground under it, est.
- `S6.prop.parasol_south_4265` (big parasol on the street's south edge): Canopy u 4098-4420, v 1286-1325; pole u 4262-4268 visible down to v ~1395. SCALE: canopy 322 px across.
- `S6.prop.goods_tray_4258` (goods on the west tray): Boxes u 4257-4295, v 916-939.

- `S6.prop.box_stack_4082` (stack of flat boxes): Stack u 4065-4100, v 1230-1259; plank u 4053-4150, v 1259-1273. It may be a load hanging from the pole (carried by the two men) rather than set down.

- `S6.road.cross_2970` (cross street at the crossroads (running north)): Both sides are receding lines (the incense shop's east eave, slope -1.04, and the corner shop's west eave and ridge, -0.91 / -1.14), so it runs due north. Its walls' feet are hidden: edge_w = the incense shop's east wall (est 240 px below its east eave, the eave height measured on the corner shop's front); edge_e = the corner shop's west wall (est 265 px below its west eave). About 11.5 m wide (820 px of u at the mouth). The centre starts on the main street's centre line (2970,1178) and is drawn along the receding direction (dx ~52). It meets the main street's north edge between (2908,790) and (3725,865). South of the main street there is no built edge: the ground between the 解 building and the inn's west wing is an open square running out over the silk's edge, so the street may continue south across it, but nothing marks it. North: it ends at the top street (S6.road.top_2814), across which the cloth shop and the clinic front it (their post feet v 180-197); it leaves the silk only through that street. Tables and a lattice cabinet under the incense shop's lean-to stand on its west margin (3200-3330).
- `S6.road.top_2814` (top street (behind the incense shop, in front of the cloth shop)): Ground is seen at (3050-3250, v 80-165) behind the incense shop's north gable (two men at a bench or rail, a man with a load) and around the cart at 3260-3420, v 30-160. edge_n: the cloth shop's and clinic's post feet (v 180-197); west of the cloth shop's west end the ground reaches higher (v ~80) and the silk's top edge. edge_s: the north range of the incense compound (est v 286), the incense shop's north gable (est 3245,440), across the cross street's mouth, the corner shop's north end (est). Its east end is hidden behind the corner shop's roof and the big tree at 4150-4500; its course beyond is unknown.
- `S6.road.main` (main street (inside the gate, through the crossroads)): No road edge is drawn as a line; edges are where the open ground stops. edge_n: the wall foot of the house behind the well (v ~800; the well stands on the street's north margin), the incense shop's south end (est v 790, behind the paper-horse stall's parasols, whose feet are at v ~850), its south-east corner (est 2908,790), then straight across the mouth of the cross street to the corner shop's south-west corner (est 3725,865), the corner shop, butcher's and 正店 fronts (column feet hidden by the crowd: est v 872, 880-885, 905; a gap of ~100 px between the butcher's and the 正店 at 4340-4441 holds only the slanting flag pole), and the wine depot's floor front (S5, est v 885-888). The 正店's slatted railings stand on the street ~95 px in front of its front (feet v ~1000). edge_s: the 解 building's north side and its slanted east board ((2045,1310) down to the silk's edge at (2165,1775); the building is S7's); open ground to the silk's edge (measured at v 1772-1785 along the section) from u ~2165 to the inn's west wing; the wing's west wall (est, along the receding direction from (3161,1775) to its NW corner (3501,1440)); then the north walls of the south-side houses, all hidden behind their own roofs, est at v 1440 to meet S5 (5600,1440). Nothing painted on the street stands lower than v ~1440 east of the wing (the boy at 3420,1395; trestles and a rider at 4800-5300, feet 1420-1440), so 1440 is the street's narrowest reading; the houses' real north walls may lie up to ~150 px lower (S5's house_5800 has porch posts with feet at v 1694-1758 under a top line at 1450): a question for the lead. Centre: through the traffic between the edges (walkers and porters at v 1100-1250 west of the crossroads; east of it midway between the fronts and v 1440, easing to S5's road end); its last point is exactly (5600,1250). The cross street S6.road.cross_2970 leaves it northward between u 2908 and 3725 (north edge).
